(dynamic workflow)","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"design","description":"Grant or revoke Claude agent access to your Design projects","argumentHint":"consent | revoke","builtin":true},{"name":"design-sync","description":"Push a React design system to claude.ai/design. This runs a converter that bundles the real component code (from Storybook or a bare package) and uploads it. Use when the user runs /design-sync or says \"sync my design system to Claude Design\".","argumentHint":"[<project hint, e.g. \"Acme DS\">]","builtin":true},{"name":"dataviz","description":"Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. When the destination is a first-party document connector (host-designated, never self-described) that renders live charts, hand it the rows (inline, or as an uploaded data file the chart cites) rather than a rendered PNG/SVG — a picture of a chart loses hover, data inspection and per-value comments. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"update-config","description":"Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"verify","description":"Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"debug","description":"Enable debug logging for this session and help diagnose issues","argumentHint":"[issue description]","builtin":true},{"name":"code-review","description":"Review the current diff, or a PR number/branch/path target, for correctness bugs (plus reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups where the model's review recipe covers them) at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud); with no level given, it reuses the level you typed last. Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review. For ultra on a GitHub.com PR target, --post asks to post the finished review’s findings to the PR as a single comment from the user’s GitHub account (not a review; the launch dialog still confirms in interactive sessions, while non-interactive mode posts on the flag alone) and --no-post hides that option.","argumentHint":"[low|medium|high|xhigh|max|ultra] [--fix] [--comment] [<pr#>|<branch>|<path>]","aliases":["review"],"builtin":true},{"name":"simplify","description":"Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.","argumentHint":"[<target>]","builtin":true},{"name":"batch","description":"Research and plan a large-scale change, then execute it in parallel across 5–30 isolated worktree agents that each open a PR.","argumentHint":"<instruction>","builtin":true},{"name":"fewer-permission-prompts","description":"Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.","argumentHint":"","builtin":true},{"name":"doctor","description":"Health-check the user's Claude Code setup and fix issues: diagnose installation health — what the `claude doctor` terminal diagnostics cover — from local data (duplicate or leftover installs, PATH, unparseable settings files, broken or colliding agent definitions, skills whose frontmatter fails to parse); find unused skills, MCP servers, and plugins versus their context cost and disable dead weight; deduplicate local CLAUDE.md files against checked-in ones; trim checked-in CLAUDE.md files by cutting content a session could derive from the codebase (directory layouts, tech-stack lists, architecture overviews) while keeping gotchas, rationale, and non-standard conventions; migrate always-loaded CLAUDE.md guidance into lazy skills and nested CLAUDE.md files; flag slow hooks and context-heavy extensions; check the installed version is current; make auto mode the default permission mode; and pre-approve frequently denied read-only commands. Use when the user asks for a doctor run, checkup, audit, tune-up, or cleanup of their Claude Code setup or configuration.","argumentHint":"","aliases":["checkup"],"builtin":true},{"name":"loop","description":"Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Claude desktop app (also called the in-app browser, the browser pane, Claude's browser, or \"your own browser\"), whose tools are named mcp__Claude_Browser__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser tool instead. It covers the pane's persistent sign-ins, tabs and preview_start, reading pages as text, site approvals, what the pane cannot open, and what to do when it cannot be reached. It is not for Claude in Chrome (mcp__claude-in-chrome__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["built-in-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:chrome-browser","description":"Read this skill before the first step that uses Claude in Chrome, the browser extension whose tools are named mcp__claude-in-chrome__* (also called Chrome, the browser extension, or the external browser) and which acts in the person's real Chrome with their own sign-ins; before those tools are turned on there may be a single enable__mcp__claude-in-chrome tool instead. It covers loading the tools in one ToolSearch call, checking the person's open tabs and working in a new tab, site permissions, GIF recordings, console logs, dialogs to avoid, and when to stop and ask. It is not for the built-in browser (mcp__Claude_Browser__* or mcp__remote-devices__Claude_Browser__* tools), which has its own skill, and it does not decide which browser to use. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["chrome-browser"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:computer-use","description":"Read this skill before the first step of any request to do something in an app on the person's own computer (Notes, Finder, System Settings, any desktop app), to look at their screen, or for \"computer use\". Computer use (desktop control) lets Claude take screenshots of the person's desktop and control it with clicks, typing and scrolling through the Claude desktop app; its tools are named mcp__computer-use__* when the session runs in the desktop app and mcp__remote-devices__computer_* when a cloud session is linked to the person's computer; before computer use is turned on for a conversation there may be no such tools, only an enable__mcp__remote-devices__computer tool, which turns it on. It covers turning it on, picking the right tool, the access flow, and the safety rules for tiered apps, links and financial actions. It is not for websites, which go through Claude in Chrome or the built-in browser and their own skills. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["computer-use"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:deep-research","description":"Use this skill when the user's prompt requires (1) researching a topic across multiple sources, comparing options or alternatives, analyzing trends or history, understanding markets or industries, or reviewing literature or studies and (2) synthesizing that research into a comprehensive, narrative report. If you're planning to search the web or internal knowledge bases, consider using this skill. This skill coordinates research subagents, so use it only when you have a tool for spawning subagents (the Agent or Task tool); otherwise, research the question directly. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":""},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docs","description":"docs (living docs people share, comment on and edit; use only when the user asks for one: names a doc, document, page, memo, spec, PRD, runbook or write-up, asks for somewhere to share or keep editing something, or says yes to your doc offer; a plan, comparison, summary or notes asked in chat stays in chat (at most a one-line doc offer); a report, status update, recap or \"something I can send them\" with no form named → ask first: reply, doc or file?; tabs hold tables and live charts too; a pasted claude.ai/code/artifact/… link may be a doc: check with docs tools first; not HTML pages, apps or plain chat answers; a .docx/.pptx/.xlsx/PDF asked for by name → that format's skill): asked for one → no docs-connector instructions in context? call the docs connector's `guide` with topic.instructions first, then create the doc (headings only, no body) before any search, file read or plan, even with files attached. Documenting code means docstrings or repo docs, not a doc. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docs"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:docx","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to create, read, edit, or manipulate Word documents (.docx) or Word templates (.dotx). Triggers include: any mention of 'Word doc', 'word document', '.docx', '.dotx', or requests to produce professional documents with formatting like tables of contents, page numbers, or letterheads. Also use when extracting or reorganizing content from .docx or .dotx files, inserting or replacing images in documents, find-and-replace in Word files, working with tracked changes or comments, or converting content into a polished Word document. If the user asks for a 'report', 'memo', 'letter', 'template', or similar deliverable as a Word or .docx file (to download, email or print), use this skill. However, if they ask for a document, page, report, memo, or notes WITHOUT naming a file format and the session offers Claude's own dedicated document or page skill or connector, use that instead. Do NOT use for PDFs, spreadsheets, Google Docs, or coding unrelated to document generation. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["docx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:import-memory","description":"Import a memory export from another AI assistant into Claude's memory — conversationally, additively, and with the content treated as data. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["import-memory"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:morning","description":"Render the user's morning brief as a styled HTML artifact, or set it up as a recurring weekday task. Use only when the user explicitly asks to run, see, or set up their morning brief, or if they invoke /morning by name. A question about their day, schedule, or calendar is not by itself a request for the brief; answer it directly instead. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["morning"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pdf","description":"Use this skill whenever the user wants to do anything with PDF files. This includes reading or extracting text/tables from PDFs, combining or merging multiple PDFs into one, splitting PDFs apart, rotating pages, adding watermarks, creating new PDFs, filling PDF forms, encrypting/decrypting PDFs, extracting images, and OCR on scanned PDFs to make them searchable. If the user mentions a .pdf file or asks to produce one, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pdf"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:pptx","description":"Use this skill any time a .pptx or .potx file is involved in any way — as input, output, or both. This includes: creating slide decks, pitch decks, or presentations as PowerPoint (.pptx) files; reading, parsing, or extracting text from any .pptx or .potx file (even if the extracted content will be used elsewhere, like in an email, summary, or creating a different type of slide deck); editing, modifying, or updating existing presentations; combining or splitting slide files; working with templates (.potx), layouts, speaker notes, or comments. Trigger whenever the user asks for a PowerPoint or .pptx file, or references a .pptx or .potx filename, regardless of what they plan to do with the content afterward. However, when the user asks for a deck, slides, a slide deck, or a presentation without naming a file format, default to using a dedicated slide-deck artifact type or a separate slides skill if this session offers one; otherwise, use this skill. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["pptx"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:setup-writing-style","description":"Learns how the user writes from their own sent messages and docs, and builds a voice profile so future drafts sound like them instead of generic AI. The profile is saved as the my-writing-style skill. Use when the user asks to set up, learn, or capture their writing voice, or complains that drafts sound generic or unlike them and no my-writing-style profile exists. Only for drafting text the user will send as themselves, not for Claude's own replies. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["setup-writing-style"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:skill-creator","description":"Create new skills, modify and improve existing skills, and measure skill performance. Use when users want to create a skill from scratch, edit, or optimize an existing skill, run evals to test a skill, benchmark skill performance with variance analysis, or optimize a skill's description for better triggering accuracy. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["skill-creator"]},{"name":"anthropic-skills:xlsx","description":"Use this skill any time a spreadsheet file is the primary input or output. This means any task where the user wants to: open, read, edit, or fix an existing .xlsx, .xlsm, .xltx, .csv, or .tsv file (e.g., adding columns, computing formulas, formatting, charting, cleaning messy data); create a new spreadsheet from scratch or from other data sources; or convert between tabular file formats. Trigger especially when the user references a spreadsheet file by name or path — even casually (like \"the xlsx in my downloads\") — and wants something done to it or produced from it. Also trigger for cleaning or restructuring messy tabular data files (malformed rows, misplaced headers, junk data) into proper spreadsheets. The deliverable must be a spreadsheet file. Do NOT trigger when the primary deliverable is a Word document, HTML report, standalone Python script, database pipeline, or Google Sheets API integration, even if tabular data is involved. (claude.ai sync)","argumentHint":"","aliases":["xlsx"]}],"uuid":"ac34811b-ba59-407b-b632-a79cd0947d6a","session_id":"ccd9465f-2ef6-4793-b56b-c218c76f8065"}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_013fiLjtCSaZYW9MnpGeZYsU","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Finance currency SSoT invariant (FARM-MEDIUM-145, FARM-HIGH-151, HR-HIGH-008).\n3\t * ============================================================================\n4\t *\n5\t * The tenant default currency has exactly ONE source of truth:\n6\t *   - farm-service: `finance_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved through\n7\t *     `FinanceSettingsService`;\n8\t *   - hr-service: `hr_payroll_cost_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved\n9\t *     through `PayrollCostSettingsService` (itself projected from the\n10\t *     farm SSoT via the `FinanceSettingsUpdated` event).\n11\t *\n12\t * The bug this freezes: independent hardcoded ISO-currency literals drift.\n13\t * `feeds`/`equipment` defaulted to `'TRY'`, `create-feeding-record.handler.ts`\n14\t * to `'NOK'`, HR `employee.entity.ts` to `'USD'` — so a tenant recording feed\n15\t * in one form and equipment in another silently booked in different currencies.\n16\t *\n17\t * # Why this is a rule and not a list\n18\t *\n19\t * It used to guard `FINANCE_HANDLER_ROOTS` recursively PLUS a hardcoded\n20\t * `NAMED_GUARDED_FILES` array, so a currency writer outside those roots was\n21\t * covered only if somebody remembered to add it. Nobody had to: the docblock\n22\t * claimed \"there is no longer a hardcoded-currency create-handler outside this\n23\t * guarded set\" and running the spec's own pattern over every\n24\t * `apps/**\\/*.handler.ts` found SEVEN — two hr payroll handlers (HR-HIGH-008,\n25\t * a real defect: `|| 'USD'` against a platform default of NOK, stamped onto\n26\t * the cross-service `PayrollProcessed` event) and five billing handlers.\n27\t * A ratchet whose coverage is a memo is not a ratchet.\n28\t *\n29\t * So the guard now scans EVERY handler and EVERY entity, and a currency\n30\t * literal is a failure unless it appears in {@link DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS} with a\n31\t * ledger finding behind it. The exemption list can only shrink: a stale entry\n32\t * (declared but no longer matching) fails, so removing the literal forces\n33\t * removing the entry rather than leaving a permanent hole.\n34\t *\n35\t * # When this spec fails\n36\t *\n37\t *   1. A handler or entity grew a `|| 'XXX'` / `?? 'XXX'` / `currency: 'XXX'`\n38\t *      / `default: 'XXX'` currency literal. Resolve it through\n39\t *      `FinanceSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` /\n40\t *      `PayrollCostSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` instead. For a\n41\t *      column default there is no correct literal at all — a default cannot\n42\t *      know the tenant — so drop it and let the NOT NULL column force the\n43\t *      writer to supply one (precedent: migration 1802200000000).\n44\t *   2. A declared exemption no longer matches. Delete the entry; the debt it\n45\t *      recorded is paid.\n46\t *   3. An exemption cites a finding the ledger has RESOLVED. The finding says\n47\t *      the work is done, so the exemption must go with it.\n48\t */\n49\timport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\n50\timport path from 'node:path';\n51\t\n52\tconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n53\tconst REGISTRY_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'docs/reviews/_registry/findings.jsonl');\n54\t\n55\t/**\n56\t * A recognised ISO 4217 currency code hardcoded on a currency-writing path:\n57\t *   - a fallback:       `|| 'USD'` / `?? \"nok\"`\n58\t *   - a direct assign:  `currency: 'TRY'`\n59\t *   - a column default: `default: 'USD'`\n60\t * The allowlist keeps the match precise (a bare `[A-Za-z]{3}` would also flag\n61\t * `'ASC'` sort orders and `'UTC'` zones).\n62\t */\n63\tconst CURRENCY_LITERAL =\n64\t  /(?:(?:\\|\\||\\?\\?)\\s*|currency:\\s*|default:\\s*)['\"](TRY|NOK|USD|EUR|GBP|SEK|DKK|CHF|JPY|CNY)['\"]/gi;\n65\t\n66\t/**\n67\t * Currency literals that are allowed to remain, each with the finding that\n68\t * tracks paying them off. NOT an exemption mechanism for new code: adding an\n69\t * entry means registering a finding with an owner and a deadline first.\n70\t */\n71\tconst DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{\n72\t  readonly file: string;\n73\t  readonly finding: string;\n74\t  readonly reason: string;\n75\t}> = [\n76\t  // BILLING-MEDIUM-016 — platform-vs-tenant currency is undeclared in billing.\n77\t  // Not asserted to be a defect: Stripe-facing platform billing in USD is a\n78\t  // defensible policy. The defect is that nothing says which reading is meant,\n79\t  // so no gate can enforce either. Declaring it is the fix; until then the\n80\t  // exemption makes the ambiguity visible instead of it being the absence of a\n81\t  // list entry.\n82\t  ...[\n83\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/change-subscription-plan.handler.ts',\n84\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-invoice.handler.ts',\n85\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-plan.handler.ts',\n86\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-subscription.handler.ts',\n87\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/query-handlers/get-tenant-billing.handler.ts',\n88\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/custom-plan.entity.ts',\n89\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/discount-code.entity.ts',\n90\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/invoice.entity.ts',\n91\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/module-price.entity.ts',\n92\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/payment.entity.ts',\n93\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/plan.entity.ts',\n94\t    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/subscription-module-item.entity.ts',\n95\t    'apps/admin-api-service/src/analytics/entities/external/invoice.entity.ts',\n96\t  ].map((file) => ({\n97\t    file,\n98\t    finding: 'BILLING-MEDIUM-016',\n99\t    reason: 'platform-vs-tenant billing currency is undeclared',\n100\t  })),\n101\t\n102\t  // FARM-MEDIUM-327 — the handlers were migrated to the settings SSoT; the DDL\n103\t  // layer they write into was not. These columns disagree with each other\n104\t  // inside one service (TRY / NOK / USD on sibling farm tables), which is the\n105\t  // FARM-MEDIUM-145 drift one layer down. No live write reaches them because\n106\t  // every handler supplies a currency, but each is a loaded trap for a\n107\t  // backfill, an admin tool or the next writer. The fix is to drop the default\n108\t  // (a default cannot know the tenant) as migration 1802200000000 did for\n109\t  // payrolls.currency — 16 migrations across 4 services plus a NOT NULL audit,\n110\t  // which is why it is tracked rather than done inline.\n111\t  ...[\n112\t    'apps/farm-service/src/chemical/entities/chemical.entity.ts',\n113\t    'apps/farm-service/src/consumable/entities/consumable.entity.ts',\n114\t    'apps/farm-service/src/equipment/entities/equipment.entity.ts',\n115\t    'apps/farm-service/src/feed/entities/feed.entity.ts',\n116\t    'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/spare-part.entity.ts',\n117\t    'apps/farm-service/src/storage/entities/purchase-order.entity.ts',\n118\t    'apps/farm-service/src/worker/entities/worker.entity.ts',\n119\t    'apps/hr-service/src/hr/entities/employee.entity.ts',\n120\t  ].map((file) => ({\n121\t    file,\n122\t    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n123\t    reason: 'entity currency column still carries a literal DDL default',\n124\t  })),\n125\t\n126\t  // FARM-MEDIUM-327, but the LIVE member of the set and the one this rule\n127\t  // found that a handler-only scan could not: `work_orders.currency` is\n128\t  // nullable with no default, while the derived `costSummary` jsonb requires a\n129\t  // non-null `currency`, so `updateCostSummary()` stamps TRY on every work\n130\t  // order created without one — whatever the tenant's currency is. An entity\n131\t  // method cannot reach FinanceSettingsService, so the fix is to derive the\n132\t  // cost summary in the handler that can, not to swap the literal.\n133\t  {\n134\t    file: 'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/work-order.entity.ts',\n135\t    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n136\t    reason: 'costSummary derives a non-null currency from a nullable column inside the entity',\n137\t  },\n138\t];\n139\t\n140\tconst EXEMPT_FILES = new Set(DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.map((entry) => entry.file));\n141\t\n142\t/** Every `*.handler.ts` and `*.entity.ts` under `apps/`, tests excluded. */\n143\tfunction listCurrencyWritingFiles(): string[] {\n144\t  const out: string[] = [];\n145\t  const walk = (current: string): void => {\n146\t    let entries: string[];\n147\t    try {\n148\t      entries = readdirSync(current);\n149\t    } catch {\n150\t      return;\n151\t    }\n152\t    for (const entry of entries) {\n153\t      const full = path.join(current, entry);\n154\t      if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) {\n155\t        if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '__tests__') continue;\n156\t        walk(full);\n157\t        continue;\n158\t      }\n159\t      if (entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) continue;\n160\t      if (/\\.(handler|entity)\\.ts$/.test(entry)) out.push(full);\n161\t    }\n162\t  };\n163\t  walk(path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'apps'));\n164\t  return out;\n165\t}\n166\t\n167\tfunction currencyLiteralsIn(file: string): string[] {\n168\t  const matches = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').match(CURRENCY_LITERAL);\n169\t  return matches ? [...new Set(matches)] : [];\n170\t}\n171\t\n172\tfunction resolvedFindingIds(): Set<string> {\n173\t  const resolved = new Set<string>();\n174\t  for (const line of readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, 'utf8').trim().split('\\n')) {\n175\t    if (!line) continue;\n176\t    const entry = JSON.parse(line) as { id?: unknown; state?: unknown };\n177\t    if (typeof entry.id === 'string' && entry.state === 'RESOLVED') resolved.add(entry.id);\n178\t  }\n179\t  return resolved;\n180\t}\n181\t\n182\tdescribe('Finance currency SSoT invariant', () => {\n183\t  const files = listCurrencyWritingFiles();\n184\t  const offenders = new Map<string, string[]>();\n185\t  for (const file of files) {\n186\t    const literals = currencyLiteralsIn(file);\n187\t    if (literals.length > 0) offenders.set(path.relative(REPO_ROOT, file), literals);\n188\t  }\n189\t\n190\t  it('scans every handler and entity under apps/ (extractor sanity)', () => {\n191\t    // A broken walker would report zero offenders and pass everything below.\n192\t    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(500);\n193\t    expect(files.some((f) => f.endsWith('create-feeding-record.handler.ts'))).toBe(true);\n194\t    expect(files.some((f) => f.endsWith('payroll.entity.ts'))).toBe(true);\n195\t  });\n196\t\n197\t  it('no handler or entity hardcodes a currency outside a declared exemption', () => {\n198\t    const undeclared = [...offenders]\n199\t      .filter(([file]) => !EXEMPT_FILES.has(file))\n200\t      .map(([file, literals]) => `${file} → ${literals.join(', ')}`);\n201\t\n202\t    expect(undeclared).toEqual([]);\n203\t  });\n204\t\n205\t  it('every declared exemption still carries a literal (the list only shrinks)', () => {\n206\t    const stale = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => !offenders.has(entry.file)).map(\n207\t      (entry) => `${entry.file} (${entry.finding}) — no currency literal left; delete this entry`,\n208\t    );\n209\t\n210\t    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n211\t  });\n212\t\n213\t  it('every exemption cites a finding the ledger still has open', () => {\n214\t    const resolved = resolvedFindingIds();\n215\t    const contradicted = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => resolved.has(entry.finding)).map(\n216\t      (entry) => `${entry.file} cites ${entry.finding}, which is RESOLVED`,\n217\t    );\n218\t\n219\t    expect(contradicted).toEqual([]);\n220\t  });\n221\t});\n222\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ccd9465f-2ef6-4793-b56b-c218c76f8065","uuid":"7a77bb1d-4c5b-4cb1-ac95-aae9fdb8c2f4","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:20:52.629Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-425c717243a6/tests/invariants/finance-currency-ssot.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Finance currency SSoT invariant (FARM-MEDIUM-145, FARM-HIGH-151, HR-HIGH-008).\n * ============================================================================\n *\n * The tenant default currency has exactly ONE source of truth:\n *   - farm-service: `finance_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved through\n *     `FinanceSettingsService`;\n *   - hr-service: `hr_payroll_cost_settings.defaultCurrency`, resolved\n *     through `PayrollCostSettingsService` (itself projected from the\n *     farm SSoT via the `FinanceSettingsUpdated` event).\n *\n * The bug this freezes: independent hardcoded ISO-currency literals drift.\n * `feeds`/`equipment` defaulted to `'TRY'`, `create-feeding-record.handler.ts`\n * to `'NOK'`, HR `employee.entity.ts` to `'USD'` — so a tenant recording feed\n * in one form and equipment in another silently booked in different currencies.\n *\n * # Why this is a rule and not a list\n *\n * It used to guard `FINANCE_HANDLER_ROOTS` recursively PLUS a hardcoded\n * `NAMED_GUARDED_FILES` array, so a currency writer outside those roots was\n * covered only if somebody remembered to add it. Nobody had to: the docblock\n * claimed \"there is no longer a hardcoded-currency create-handler outside this\n * guarded set\" and running the spec's own pattern over every\n * `apps/**\\/*.handler.ts` found SEVEN — two hr payroll handlers (HR-HIGH-008,\n * a real defect: `|| 'USD'` against a platform default of NOK, stamped onto\n * the cross-service `PayrollProcessed` event) and five billing handlers.\n * A ratchet whose coverage is a memo is not a ratchet.\n *\n * So the guard now scans EVERY handler and EVERY entity, and a currency\n * literal is a failure unless it appears in {@link DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS} with a\n * ledger finding behind it. The exemption list can only shrink: a stale entry\n * (declared but no longer matching) fails, so removing the literal forces\n * removing the entry rather than leaving a permanent hole.\n *\n * # When this spec fails\n *\n *   1. A handler or entity grew a `|| 'XXX'` / `?? 'XXX'` / `currency: 'XXX'`\n *      / `default: 'XXX'` currency literal. Resolve it through\n *      `FinanceSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` /\n *      `PayrollCostSettingsService.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(...)` instead. For a\n *      column default there is no correct literal at all — a default cannot\n *      know the tenant — so drop it and let the NOT NULL column force the\n *      writer to supply one (precedent: migration 1802200000000).\n *   2. A declared exemption no longer matches. Delete the entry; the debt it\n *      recorded is paid.\n *   3. An exemption cites a finding the ledger has RESOLVED. The finding says\n *      the work is done, so the exemption must go with it.\n */\nimport { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst REGISTRY_PATH = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'docs/reviews/_registry/findings.jsonl');\n\n/**\n * A recognised ISO 4217 currency code hardcoded on a currency-writing path:\n *   - a fallback:       `|| 'USD'` / `?? \"nok\"`\n *   - a direct assign:  `currency: 'TRY'`\n *   - a column default: `default: 'USD'`\n * The allowlist keeps the match precise (a bare `[A-Za-z]{3}` would also flag\n * `'ASC'` sort orders and `'UTC'` zones).\n */\nconst CURRENCY_LITERAL =\n  /(?:(?:\\|\\||\\?\\?)\\s*|currency:\\s*|default:\\s*)['\"](TRY|NOK|USD|EUR|GBP|SEK|DKK|CHF|JPY|CNY)['\"]/gi;\n\n/**\n * Currency literals that are allowed to remain, each with the finding that\n * tracks paying them off. NOT an exemption mechanism for new code: adding an\n * entry means registering a finding with an owner and a deadline first.\n */\nconst DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{\n  readonly file: string;\n  readonly finding: string;\n  readonly reason: string;\n}> = [\n  // BILLING-MEDIUM-016 — platform-vs-tenant currency is undeclared in billing.\n  // Not asserted to be a defect: Stripe-facing platform billing in USD is a\n  // defensible policy. The defect is that nothing says which reading is meant,\n  // so no gate can enforce either. Declaring it is the fix; until then the\n  // exemption makes the ambiguity visible instead of it being the absence of a\n  // list entry.\n  ...[\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/change-subscription-plan.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-invoice.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-plan.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/handlers/create-subscription.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/query-handlers/get-tenant-billing.handler.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/custom-plan.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/discount-code.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/invoice.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/module-price.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/payment.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/plan.entity.ts',\n    'apps/billing-service/src/billing/entities/subscription-module-item.entity.ts',\n    'apps/admin-api-service/src/analytics/entities/external/invoice.entity.ts',\n  ].map((file) => ({\n    file,\n    finding: 'BILLING-MEDIUM-016',\n    reason: 'platform-vs-tenant billing currency is undeclared',\n  })),\n\n  // FARM-MEDIUM-327 — the handlers were migrated to the settings SSoT; the DDL\n  // layer they write into was not. These columns disagree with each other\n  // inside one service (TRY / NOK / USD on sibling farm tables), which is the\n  // FARM-MEDIUM-145 drift one layer down. No live write reaches them because\n  // every handler supplies a currency, but each is a loaded trap for a\n  // backfill, an admin tool or the next writer. The fix is to drop the default\n  // (a default cannot know the tenant) as migration 1802200000000 did for\n  // payrolls.currency — 16 migrations across 4 services plus a NOT NULL audit,\n  // which is why it is tracked rather than done inline.\n  ...[\n    'apps/farm-service/src/chemical/entities/chemical.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/consumable/entities/consumable.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/equipment/entities/equipment.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/feed/entities/feed.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/spare-part.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/storage/entities/purchase-order.entity.ts',\n    'apps/farm-service/src/worker/entities/worker.entity.ts',\n    'apps/hr-service/src/hr/entities/employee.entity.ts',\n  ].map((file) => ({\n    file,\n    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n    reason: 'entity currency column still carries a literal DDL default',\n  })),\n\n  // FARM-MEDIUM-327, but the LIVE member of the set and the one this rule\n  // found that a handler-only scan could not: `work_orders.currency` is\n  // nullable with no default, while the derived `costSummary` jsonb requires a\n  // non-null `currency`, so `updateCostSummary()` stamps TRY on every work\n  // order created without one — whatever the tenant's currency is. An entity\n  // method cannot reach FinanceSettingsService, so the fix is to derive the\n  // cost summary in the handler that can, not to swap the literal.\n  {\n    file: 'apps/farm-service/src/maintenance/entities/work-order.entity.ts',\n    finding: 'FARM-MEDIUM-327',\n    reason: 'costSummary derives a non-null currency from a nullable column inside the entity',\n  },\n];\n\nconst EXEMPT_FILES = new Set(DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.map((entry) => entry.file));\n\n/** Every `*.handler.ts` and `*.entity.ts` under `apps/`, tests excluded. */\nfunction listCurrencyWritingFiles(): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  const walk = (current: string): void => {\n    let entries: string[];\n    try {\n      entries = readdirSync(current);\n    } catch {\n      return;\n    }\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      const full = path.join(current, entry);\n      if (statSync(full).isDirectory()) {\n        if (entry === 'node_modules' || entry === '__tests__') continue;\n        walk(full);\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (entry.endsWith('.spec.ts')) continue;\n      if (/\\.(handler|entity)\\.ts$/.test(entry)) out.push(full);\n    }\n  };\n  walk(path.resolve(REPO_ROOT, 'apps'));\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction currencyLiteralsIn(file: string): string[] {\n  const matches = readFileSync(file, 'utf8').match(CURRENCY_LITERAL);\n  return matches ? [...new Set(matches)] : [];\n}\n\nfunction resolvedFindingIds(): Set<string> {\n  const resolved = new Set<string>();\n  for (const line of readFileSync(REGISTRY_PATH, 'utf8').trim().split('\\n')) {\n    if (!line) continue;\n    const entry = JSON.parse(line) as { id?: unknown; state?: unknown };\n    if (typeof entry.id === 'string' && entry.state === 'RESOLVED') resolved.add(entry.id);\n  }\n  return resolved;\n}\n\ndescribe('Finance currency SSoT invariant', () => {\n  const files = listCurrencyWritingFiles();\n  const offenders = new Map<string, string[]>();\n  for (const file of files) {\n    const literals = currencyLiteralsIn(file);\n    if (literals.length > 0) offenders.set(path.relative(REPO_ROOT, file), literals);\n  }\n\n  it('scans every handler and entity under apps/ (extractor sanity)', () => {\n    // A broken walker would report zero offenders and pass everything below.\n    expect(files.length).toBeGreaterThan(500);\n    expect(files.some((f) => f.endsWith('create-feeding-record.handler.ts'))).toBe(true);\n    expect(files.some((f) => f.endsWith('payroll.entity.ts'))).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('no handler or entity hardcodes a currency outside a declared exemption', () => {\n    const undeclared = [...offenders]\n      .filter(([file]) => !EXEMPT_FILES.has(file))\n      .map(([file, literals]) => `${file} → ${literals.join(', ')}`);\n\n    expect(undeclared).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every declared exemption still carries a literal (the list only shrinks)', () => {\n    const stale = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => !offenders.has(entry.file)).map(\n      (entry) => `${entry.file} (${entry.finding}) — no currency literal left; delete this entry`,\n    );\n\n    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every exemption cites a finding the ledger still has open', () => {\n    const resolved = resolvedFindingIds();\n    const contradicted = DECLARED_EXEMPTIONS.filter((entry) => resolved.has(entry.finding)).map(\n      (entry) => `${entry.file} cites ${entry.finding}, which is RESOLVED`,\n    );\n\n    expect(contradicted).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":222,"startLine":1,"totalLines":222}}}

COLUMN SET NOT NULL', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" SET NOT NULL`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(input);\n    });\n\n    it('preserves schema-qualified table names through ADD COLUMN rewrite', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"tenant_abc123def456abc1\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"newCol\" varchar`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"tenant_abc123def456abc1\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"newCol\" varchar`,\n      );\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('end-to-end pipeline (whitelist + idempotency)', () => {\n    it('keeps and rewrites a missing-column ADD scenario', () => {\n      const sql = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(true);\n      expect(makeIdempotent(sql)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('rejects the deploy 5 PK conflict scenario before idempotency runs', () => {\n      const sql = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"scheduling_settings\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"PK_x\" PRIMARY KEY (\"tenantId\")`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it('rejects the deploy 4 DROP TYPE cascade scenario', () => {\n      const sql = `DROP TYPE \"hr\".\"work_week_day\"`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":188,"startLine":1,"totalLines":188}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Li3W2eBzqvRe1pvhJAA9LA","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Platform-wide invariant — MIGRATION-SPEC-QUARANTINE:\n3\t *\n4\t * `.spec.ts` files MUST NOT live directly inside any service's\n5\t * top-level migration directory. Test specs belong in a sibling\n6\t * `__tests__/` subdirectory — that is where the codebase-wide\n7\t * convention puts every other test, and where\n8\t * `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` already excludes them.\n9\t *\n10\t * # Why this invariant exists\n11\t *\n12\t * `apps/db-migrate` is a single CLI that compiles every service's\n13\t * migration source tree into one bundle (see schema-registry.ts).\n14\t * Its `tsconfig.build.json` `include` block pulls each service's\n15\t * `migrations/` directory across `..` boundaries, so a bare\n16\t * `**\\/*.spec.ts` exclude pattern (anchored to db-migrate's tsconfig\n17\t * directory) does NOT reach the cross-tree spec files. The build\n18\t * scope discovers a spec, the spec uses Jest globals, and the\n19\t * production tsconfig deliberately omits `@types/jest` from `types`\n20\t * so that production bundles do not advertise Jest globals — the\n21\t * compile then fails with TS2593 / TS2304.\n22\t *\n23\t * The first instances of this regression were\n24\t * `apps/auth-service/src/migrations/*.spec.ts` and\n25\t * `apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations/*.spec.ts` (commit\n26\t * efddd723), which broke `build` and `Frontend Lighthouse CI` jobs\n27\t * on `main`. The fix moved the specs into `__tests__/` and added\n28\t * cross-tree exclude entries to `tsconfig.build.json` as a Tier-3\n29\t * safety net.\n30\t *\n31\t * This invariant locks in the Tier-1 shape of the fix: any future\n32\t * `*.spec.ts` placed directly inside a top-level `migrations/`\n33\t * directory of a service is a regression and is rejected at PR\n34\t * time, before it reaches the build scope.\n35\t *\n36\t * # Detection contract\n37\t *\n38\t * Walk every service under `apps/<svc>/src/`. For any directory whose\n39\t * leaf segment is `migrations` (i.e. `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/` or\n40\t * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/`), assert that no immediate\n41\t * `.spec.ts` child file exists. Spec files inside a nested\n42\t * `__tests__/` are accepted — they are the canonical placement.\n43\t *\n44\t * # What this invariant does NOT check\n45\t *\n46\t *   - It does not enforce that test specs exist for every migration —\n47\t *     test coverage is a separate concern.\n48\t *   - It does not police `.test.ts` files (the same rule applies in\n49\t *     spirit; the regression only ever surfaced for `.spec.ts` and\n50\t *     `tsconfig.build.json` already covers `**\\/*.test.ts` per\n51\t *     migration tree as a belt-and-braces measure).\n52\t *   - It does not validate that `tsconfig.build.json`'s exclude\n53\t *     entries are paired with each include entry — that pairing is\n54\t *     enforced by the build itself: any future regression compiles\n55\t *     the spec into the bundle and trips TS2593, which `tsc -p\n56\t *     ...tsconfig.build.json` surfaces as a hard failure.\n57\t *\n58\t * # Closure\n59\t *\n60\t * Closes: orphan finding tracked by the build-failure plan at\n61\t * /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md §6 (\"Long-term invariant\").\n62\t */\n63\t\n64\timport { readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\n65\timport path from 'node:path';\n66\t\n67\tconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n68\tconst APPS_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n69\t\n70\t/**\n71\t * Recursively walk a directory and yield absolute paths whose leaf\n72\t * directory name is `migrations`. Stops descending into hidden or\n73\t * `node_modules` directories.\n74\t */\n75\tfunction findMigrationDirs(root: string): string[] {\n76\t  const out: string[] = [];\n77\t  const stack: string[] = [root];\n78\t  while (stack.length > 0) {\n79\t    const dir = stack.pop();\n80\t    if (!dir) continue;\n81\t    let entries: string[];\n82\t    try {\n83\t      entries = readdirSync(dir);\n84\t    } catch {\n85\t      continue;\n86\t    }\n87\t    for (const entry of entries) {\n88\t      if (entry.startsWith('.') || entry === 'node_modules') continue;\n89\t      const full = path.join(dir, entry);\n90\t      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n91\t      try {\n92\t        st = statSync(full);\n93\t      } catch {\n94\t        continue;\n95\t      }\n96\t      if (!st.isDirectory()) continue;\n97\t      if (entry === 'migrations') {\n98\t        out.push(full);\n99\t        // Do NOT descend into `migrations/` itself for further\n100\t        // discovery — nested `migrations/` directories are not a\n101\t        // pattern in this codebase, and descending would re-flag\n102\t        // the same directory on its child path.\n103\t        continue;\n104\t      }\n105\t      stack.push(full);\n106\t    }\n107\t  }\n108\t  return out;\n109\t}\n110\t\n111\t/**\n112\t * List immediate `.spec.ts` children of a migrations/ directory.\n113\t * Files inside a nested `__tests__/` are NOT immediate children\n114\t * and are not returned.\n115\t */\n116\tfunction listTopLevelSpecs(migrationsDir: string): string[] {\n117\t  let entries: string[];\n118\t  try {\n119\t    entries = readdirSync(migrationsDir);\n120\t  } catch {\n121\t    return [];\n122\t  }\n123\t  return entries\n124\t    .filter((e) => e.endsWith('.spec.ts'))\n125\t    .filter((e) => {\n126\t      const full = path.join(migrationsDir, e);\n127\t      try {\n128\t        return statSync(full).isFile();\n129\t      } catch {\n130\t        return false;\n131\t      }\n132\t    })\n133\t    .map((e) => path.join(migrationsDir, e));\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\tdescribe('INVARIANT: migration spec quarantine', () => {\n137\t  it('asserts no .spec.ts file lives directly inside any service migrations/ directory', () => {\n138\t    let services: string[];\n139\t    try {\n140\t      services = readdirSync(APPS_DIR);\n141\t    } catch (err) {\n142\t      throw new Error(\n143\t        `apps/ directory not readable at ${APPS_DIR}: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n144\t      );\n145\t    }\n146\t\n147\t    const violations: string[] = [];\n148\t    for (const svc of services) {\n149\t      const svcSrc = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src');\n150\t      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n151\t      try {\n152\t        st = statSync(svcSrc);\n153\t      } catch {\n154\t        continue; // service has no src/ — not applicable\n155\t      }\n156\t      if (!st.isDirectory()) continue;\n157\t\n158\t      const migrationDirs = findMigrationDirs(svcSrc);\n159\t      for (const dir of migrationDirs) {\n160\t        const specs = listTopLevelSpecs(dir);\n161\t        for (const spec of specs) {\n162\t          violations.push(path.relative(REPO_ROOT, spec));\n163\t        }\n164\t      }\n165\t    }\n166\t\n167\t    if (violations.length > 0) {\n168\t      const lines = violations.map((v) => `  - ${v}`).join('\\n');\n169\t      throw new Error(\n170\t        [\n171\t          'Found .spec.ts file(s) placed directly inside a service migrations/ directory.',\n172\t          'These break `db-migrate:build:production` because the db-migrate tsconfig',\n173\t          'pulls every service migrations/ tree across `..` boundaries while keeping',\n174\t          '`@types/jest` deliberately out of `types` (production bundles must not',\n175\t          'advertise Jest globals). Move each spec into a sibling `__tests__/`',\n176\t          'subdirectory:',\n177\t          '',\n178\t          lines,\n179\t          '',\n180\t          'Example:',\n181\t          '  apps/<svc>/src/migrations/2026-foo.spec.ts',\n182\t          '   -> apps/<svc>/src/migrations/__tests__/2026-foo.spec.ts',\n183\t          '',\n184\t          'See /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md for the full rationale,',\n185\t          'and `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` for the matching exclude',\n186\t          'entries that act as the Tier-3 safety net.',\n187\t        ].join('\\n'),\n188\t      );\n189\t    }\n190\t  });\n191\t});\n192\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ccd9465f-2ef6-4793-b56b-c218c76f8065","uuid":"3d37ca0e-ae8f-4b91-afa9-3a09adf4f37c","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:21:51.545Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-425c717243a6/tests/invariants/migration-spec-quarantine.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Platform-wide invariant — MIGRATION-SPEC-QUARANTINE:\n *\n * `.spec.ts` files MUST NOT live directly inside any service's\n * top-level migration directory. Test specs belong in a sibling\n * `__tests__/` subdirectory — that is where the codebase-wide\n * convention puts every other test, and where\n * `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` already excludes them.\n *\n * # Why this invariant exists\n *\n * `apps/db-migrate` is a single CLI that compiles every service's\n * migration source tree into one bundle (see schema-registry.ts).\n * Its `tsconfig.build.json` `include` block pulls each service's\n * `migrations/` directory across `..` boundaries, so a bare\n * `**\\/*.spec.ts` exclude pattern (anchored to db-migrate's tsconfig\n * directory) does NOT reach the cross-tree spec files. The build\n * scope discovers a spec, the spec uses Jest globals, and the\n * production tsconfig deliberately omits `@types/jest` from `types`\n * so that production bundles do not advertise Jest globals — the\n * compile then fails with TS2593 / TS2304.\n *\n * The first instances of this regression were\n * `apps/auth-service/src/migrations/*.spec.ts` and\n * `apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations/*.spec.ts` (commit\n * efddd723), which broke `build` and `Frontend Lighthouse CI` jobs\n * on `main`. The fix moved the specs into `__tests__/` and added\n * cross-tree exclude entries to `tsconfig.build.json` as a Tier-3\n * safety net.\n *\n * This invariant locks in the Tier-1 shape of the fix: any future\n * `*.spec.ts` placed directly inside a top-level `migrations/`\n * directory of a service is a regression and is rejected at PR\n * time, before it reaches the build scope.\n *\n * # Detection contract\n *\n * Walk every service under `apps/<svc>/src/`. For any directory whose\n * leaf segment is `migrations` (i.e. `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/` or\n * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/`), assert that no immediate\n * `.spec.ts` child file exists. Spec files inside a nested\n * `__tests__/` are accepted — they are the canonical placement.\n *\n * # What this invariant does NOT check\n *\n *   - It does not enforce that test specs exist for every migration —\n *     test coverage is a separate concern.\n *   - It does not police `.test.ts` files (the same rule applies in\n *     spirit; the regression only ever surfaced for `.spec.ts` and\n *     `tsconfig.build.json` already covers `**\\/*.test.ts` per\n *     migration tree as a belt-and-braces measure).\n *   - It does not validate that `tsconfig.build.json`'s exclude\n *     entries are paired with each include entry — that pairing is\n *     enforced by the build itself: any future regression compiles\n *     the spec into the bundle and trips TS2593, which `tsc -p\n *     ...tsconfig.build.json` surfaces as a hard failure.\n *\n * # Closure\n *\n * Closes: orphan finding tracked by the build-failure plan at\n * /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md §6 (\"Long-term invariant\").\n */\n\nimport { readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst APPS_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n\n/**\n * Recursively walk a directory and yield absolute paths whose leaf\n * directory name is `migrations`. Stops descending into hidden or\n * `node_modules` directories.\n */\nfunction findMigrationDirs(root: string): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  const stack: string[] = [root];\n  while (stack.length > 0) {\n    const dir = stack.pop();\n    if (!dir) continue;\n    let entries: string[];\n    try {\n      entries = readdirSync(dir);\n    } catch {\n      continue;\n    }\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      if (entry.startsWith('.') || entry === 'node_modules') continue;\n      const full = path.join(dir, entry);\n      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n      try {\n        st = statSync(full);\n      } catch {\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (!st.isDirectory()) continue;\n      if (entry === 'migrations') {\n        out.push(full);\n        // Do NOT descend into `migrations/` itself for further\n        // discovery — nested `migrations/` directories are not a\n        // pattern in this codebase, and descending would re-flag\n        // the same directory on its child path.\n        continue;\n      }\n      stack.push(full);\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/**\n * List immediate `.spec.ts` children of a migrations/ directory.\n * Files inside a nested `__tests__/` are NOT immediate children\n * and are not returned.\n */\nfunction listTopLevelSpecs(migrationsDir: string): string[] {\n  let entries: string[];\n  try {\n    entries = readdirSync(migrationsDir);\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return entries\n    .filter((e) => e.endsWith('.spec.ts'))\n    .filter((e) => {\n      const full = path.join(migrationsDir, e);\n      try {\n        return statSync(full).isFile();\n      } catch {\n        return false;\n      }\n    })\n    .map((e) => path.join(migrationsDir, e));\n}\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT: migration spec quarantine', () => {\n  it('asserts no .spec.ts file lives directly inside any service migrations/ directory', () => {\n    let services: string[];\n    try {\n      services = readdirSync(APPS_DIR);\n    } catch (err) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `apps/ directory not readable at ${APPS_DIR}: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    const violations: string[] = [];\n    for (const svc of services) {\n      const svcSrc = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src');\n      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n      try {\n        st = statSync(svcSrc);\n      } catch {\n        continue; // service has no src/ — not applicable\n      }\n      if (!st.isDirectory()) continue;\n\n      const migrationDirs = findMigrationDirs(svcSrc);\n      for (const dir of migrationDirs) {\n        const specs = listTopLevelSpecs(dir);\n        for (const spec of specs) {\n          violations.push(path.relative(REPO_ROOT, spec));\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (violations.length > 0) {\n      const lines = violations.map((v) => `  - ${v}`).join('\\n');\n      throw new Error(\n        [\n          'Found .spec.ts file(s) placed directly inside a service migrations/ directory.',\n          'These break `db-migrate:build:production` because the db-migrate tsconfig',\n          'pulls every service migrations/ tree across `..` boundaries while keeping',\n          '`@types/jest` deliberately out of `types` (production bundles must not',\n          'advertise Jest globals). Move each spec into a sibling `__tests__/`',\n          'subdirectory:',\n          '',\n          lines,\n          '',\n          'Example:',\n          '  apps/<svc>/src/migrations/2026-foo.spec.ts',\n          '   -> apps/<svc>/src/migrations/__tests__/2026-foo.spec.ts',\n          '',\n          'See /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md for the full rationale,',\n          'and `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` for the matching exclude',\n          'entries that act as the Tier-3 safety net.',\n        ].join('\\n'),\n      );\n    }\n  });\n});\n","numLines":192,"startLine":1,"totalLines":192}}}

The tenant default is projected from the\n      // farm finance_settings SSoT into hr_payroll_cost_settings and read\n      // through PayrollCostSettingsService, exactly as CreateEmployeeHandler\n      // does. The literal that used to sit here was 'USD' while the platform\n      // default is NOK (HR_PLATFORM_DEFAULT_CURRENCY), so an employee row\n      // without a currency produced a USD payroll for a NOK tenant — and\n      // ApprovePayrollHandler then stamped that currency onto the\n      // PayrollProcessed event crossing into the finance ledger.\n      const defaultCurrency = await this.payrollCostSettings.getDefaultCurrencyInTx(\n        queryRunner.manager,\n        tenantId,\n      );\n      const currency = input.currency || employee.currency || defaultCurrency;\n\n      // Auto-compute earnings from attendance-based hours if not explicitly provided\n      let effectiveEarnings = input.earnings;\n      if (autoComputedEarnings && input.earnings.baseSalary === 0) {\n        const hourlyRate = Money.of(employee.baseSalary, currency).divide(160);\n        const regularPay = hourlyRate.multiply(workHoursData.regularHours);\n        const overtimePay = hourlyRate\n          .multiply(workHoursData.overtimeHours || 0)\n          .multiply(CreatePayrollHandler.OVERTIME_RATE);\n        effectiveEarnings = {\n          baseSalary: regularPay.toMinorUnits() / 100,\n          overtime: overtimePay.toMinorUnits() / 100,\n          bonus: input.earnings.bonus || 0,\n          commission: input.earnings.commission || 0,\n          allowances: input.earnings.allowances || 0,\n        };\n      }\n\n      // Calculate earnings breakdown using Money (no float arithmetic)\n      const earnings: EarningsBreakdown = {\n        baseSalary: effectiveEarnings.baseSalary,\n        overtime: effectiveEarnings.overtime || 0,\n        bonus: effectiveEarnings.bonus || 0,\n        commission: effectiveEarnings.commission || 0,\n        allowances: effectiveEarnings.allowances || 0,\n        grossPay: moneyAdd(\n          currency,\n          effectiveEarnings.baseSalary,\n          effectiveEarnings.overtime || 0,\n          effectiveEarnings.bonus || 0,\n          effectiveEarnings.commission || 0,\n          effectiveEarnings.allowances || 0,\n        ),\n      };\n\n      // Calculate deductions breakdown using Money (no float arithmetic)\n      const earnings,\n        deductions,\n        netPay,\n        currency,\n        status: PayrollStatus.DRAFT,\n        notes: input.notes,\n        createdBy: userId,\n        updatedBy: userId,\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Whi1pXwPnM1ntJvcyym2U1","type":"tool_result","content":"60\t#   1. Filename convention — every migration file name must match\n61\t#      `<13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts`.\n62\t#   2. Class name consistency — the exported class inside must be\n63\t#      named `<PascalCaseName><timestamp>` and match the file name.\n64\t#   3. Global timestamp uniqueness — no two migrations anywhere in the\n65\t#      repo may share the same timestamp. Collisions would produce\n66\t#      undefined ordering under TypeORM's migration runner.\n67\t#   4. Syntax / type check — each migration file compiles under\n68\t#      `tsc --noEmit` against the service's `tsconfig.build.json` so\n69\t#      structural errors (missing imports, bad decorator usage, typos\n70\t#      in table names as SQL string literals — the latter caught only\n71\t#      at runtime but the former all caught here) surface at PR time\n72\t#      instead of at deploy time.\n73\t#\n74\t# The job finishes in well under a minute and has no external\n75\t# dependencies: no postgres, no docker, no native binaries, no NX.\n76\t# It cannot hang.\n77\t#\n78\t# Real integration-level migration validation — i.e. \"does the\n79\t# migration actually run against a database\" — happens at deploy time\n80\t# via `MigrationRunnerService.onApplicationBootstrap()` and the\n81\t# deploy workflow's `Verify deployment` health check. Two different\n82\t# quality gates at two different layers, neither duplicating the\n83\t# other's work.\n84\t#\n85\t# SEC-CI-002: All third-party actions pinned to full commit SHAs.\n86\t# =============================================================================\n87\t\n88\ton:\n89\t  pull_request:\n90\t    paths:\n91\t      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n92\t      - 'apps/*/src/**/*.entity.ts'\n93\t      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n94\t      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n95\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n96\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n97\t      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n98\t  push:\n99\t    branches: [main, develop]\n100\t    paths:\n101\t      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n102\t      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n103\t      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n104\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n105\t      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n106\t      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n107\t  # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — nightly run catches drift that touched zero\n108\t  # path-filtered files but introduced runtime drift (e.g. an\n109\t  # init-script change merged through a different filename, or a"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ccd9465f-2ef6-4793-b56b-c218c76f8065","uuid":"46eae06d-3978-4df7-a2b3-72bb3c5e381e","timestamp":"2026-09-19T14:23:40.411Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-425c717243a6/.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml","content":"#   1. Class name consistency — the exported class inside must be\n#      named `<PascalCaseName><timestamp>` and match the file name.\n#   3. Global timestamp uniqueness — no two migrations anywhere in the\n#      repo may share the same timestamp. Collisions would produce\n#      undefined ordering under TypeORM's migration runner.\n#   4. Syntax / type check — each migration file compiles under\n#      `tsc --noEmit` against the service's `tsconfig.build.json` so\n#      structural errors (missing imports, bad decorator usage, typos\n#      in table names as SQL string literals — the latter caught only\n#      at runtime but the former all caught here) surface at PR time\n#      instead of at deploy time.\n#\n# The job finishes in well under a minute and has no external\n# dependencies: no postgres, no docker, no native binaries, no NX.\n# It cannot hang.\n#\n# Real integration-level migration validation — i.e. \"does the\n# migration actually run against a database\" — happens at deploy time\n# via `MigrationRunnerService.onApplicationBootstrap()` and the\n# deploy workflow's `Verify deployment` health check. Two different\n# quality gates at two different layers, neither duplicating the\n# other's work.\n#\n# SEC-CI-002: All third-party actions pinned to full commit SHAs.\n# =============================================================================\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    paths:\n      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n      - 'apps/*/src/**/*.entity.ts'\n      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n  push:\n    branches: [main, develop]\n    paths:\n      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n      - 'infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**'\n      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/tenant-clone-parity.spec.ts'\n      - 'e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts'\n      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n  # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — nightly run catches drift that touched zero\n  # path-filtered files but introduced runtime drift (e.g. an\n  # init-script change merged through a different filename, or a","numLines":50,"startLine":60,"totalLines":777}}}

The literal observation is accurate: no spec imports DropPayrollCurrencyDefault1802200000000 (the class name occurs only inside the migration), apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations/__tests__/ does not exist, the one adjacent spec (database/__tests__/sync-hr-entities-to-db.spec.ts:24) targets a different migration, and the two mentions of 1802200000000 in tests/invariants/finance-currency-ssot.spec.ts (lines 43, 108) are comments with no assertion behind them. The concern the rule encodes — a hazardous migration shipping unverified — is absent. (1) DDL outcome: the migration declares postCondition() (lines 37-47) asserting payrolls.currency has no default; apps/db-migrate/src/migration-orchestrator.ts:284-306 executes it and rolls back on false/throw; .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml triggers on apps/*/src/**/migrations/** (lines 91, 101) and runs the real db-migrate entrypoint against a fresh Postgres (lines 424, 568), so the probe ran at PR time for this file and runs at every deploy through migration-runner.service.ts:700-736. (2) Behavioral invariant: DROP DEFAULT changes only what an INSERT omitting currency does; the sole INSERT path into payrolls is create-payroll.handler.ts:194-218, which supplies currency resolved at lines 118-122, and create-payroll.handler.spec.ts:152-195 asserts that resolution (tenant default via getDefaultCurrencyInTx, employee currency, explicit input; never 'USD'). (3) Regression guard: finance-currency-ssot.spec.ts:64,194,202 scans payroll.entity.ts (not exempt) and fails on any default:'<ISO>' literal returning. (4) Convention: migration-spec-quarantine.spec.ts:44-47 records that per-migration spec files are not enforced; no numeric hr-service migration has an importing spec, so the missing 'signal' is the repository baseline, not a deviation of this file.
